. Cloud cover 
. Redirecting to 
. These are real published advisories for your area. StrataWx flies the observed weather rather than the advisory, but one you are inside is not ignored: it raises the turbulence and icing in your hazard readout, and while StrataWx is drawing the sky an IFR or mountain-obscuration advisory also holds injected visibility down to IFR.
. This does not route injection: both in-sim panels still poll this same engine.
